ebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}=U_{(1)}^{\nu\mu}.

So we just need to study the rest of the expression (
2.40
). Using the coordinate formula (
1.8
) for the equation
\mathrm{d}F=0
, which reads
\partial_{\nu}F_{\alpha\lambda}-\partial_{\alpha}F_{\nu\lambda}+\partial_{\lambda}F_{\nu\alpha}=0
, we can prove that
F^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\nu}F_{\alpha\lambda}=F^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}F_{\nu\lambda}/2
— indeed, first using the antisymmetry of
(F^{\alpha\beta})
and
(F_{\alpha\beta})
and then relabeling indices on the second term, we have:

F^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\nu}F_{\alpha\lambda}=F^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}F_{\nu\lambda}-F^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\lambda}F_{\nu\alpha}=F^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}F_{\nu\lambda}-F^{\lambda\nu}\partial_{\lambda}F_{\alpha\nu}=F^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}F_{\nu\lambda}-F^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\nu}F_{\alpha\lambda},

and now moving
F^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\nu}F_{\alpha\lambda}
to the left side yields the claimed identity. So let us apply this on the leftover term in (
2.40
):

\displaystyle-\eta^{\mu\alpha}F^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\nu}F_{\alpha\lambda}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\mu\alpha}F^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}F_{\nu\lambda}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\mu\alpha}F^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}F_{\nu\lambda}-\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\mu\alpha}F^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}F_{\nu\lambda}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\mu\alpha}F^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}F_{\nu\lambda}-\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\mu\alpha}F_{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}F^{\nu\lambda}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\mu\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}\big(F^{\nu\lambda}F_{\nu\lambda}\big)

\displaystyle=\partial_{\nu}U^{\mu\nu}_{(2)}\qquad\text{where}\quad\boxed{U^{\mu\nu}_{(2)}:=-\frac{1}{4}\eta^{\mu\nu}F^{\alpha\beta}F_{\alpha\beta}}.

Thus we have proved
\mathchoice{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}\partial_{\nu}F^{\nu\lambda}=\partial_{\nu}\big(U^{\mu\nu}_{(1)}+U^{\mu\nu}_{(2)}\big)
for symmetric expressions
U_{(j)}
. Defining
4\pi T^{\mu\nu}_{\mathrm{(M)}}
as
U^{\mu\nu}_{(1)}+U^{\mu\nu}_{(2)}
gives the first term of (
2.39
) as claimed in the proposition.

It remains to derive
T^{\mu\nu}_{\mathrm{(BLTP)}}
. The beginning of the computation is analogous to the above:

\displaystyle\mathchoice{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}\partial_{\nu}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\nu\lambda}

\displaystyle=\partial_{\nu}\big(\mathchoice{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\nu\lambda}\big)-(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\nu}\mathchoice{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}

\displaystyle=\partial_{\nu}V^{\mu\nu}_{(1)}-\eta^{\mu\alpha}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\nu}F_{\alpha\lambda}\qquad\text{where}\quad\boxed{V^{\mu\nu}_{(1)}:=\mathchoice{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\nu\lambda}}

\displaystyle=\partial_{\nu}V^{\mu\nu}_{(1)}-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\mu\alpha}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}F_{\nu\lambda}.

Note that
V_{(1)}
is
not
symmetric, but we choose to keep it as a piece of our desired tensor because its counterpart with
\mu
and
\nu
exchanged will appear later. Next we “integrate by parts” again in the leftover term, this time with respect to
\partial_{\alpha}
:

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\mu\alpha}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}F_{\nu\lambda}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\mu\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}\big((\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\nu\lambda}F_{\nu\lambda}\big)+\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\mu\alpha}F_{\nu\lambda}\partial_{\alpha}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\nu\lambda}

\displaystyle=\partial_{\nu}V^{\mu\nu}_{(2)}+\frac{1}{2}F_{\nu\lambda}\partial^{\mu}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\nu\lambda}\qquad\text{where}\quad\boxed{V^{\mu\nu}_{(2)}:=-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\mu\nu}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\alpha\beta}F_{\alpha\beta}}.

To continue with the leftover term, we apply formula (
1.8
) for
\mathrm{d}
(but with raised indices, which is allowed in an inertial coordinate system) and move the
\nu
derivative over:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}F_{\nu\lambda}\partial^{\mu}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\nu\lambda}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}F_{\nu\lambda}\partial^{\mu}\big(\partial^{\nu}(\delta F)^{\lambda}-\partial^{\lambda}(\delta F)^{\nu}\big)

\displaystyle=F_{\nu\lambda}\partial^{\mu}\partial^{\nu}(\delta F)^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\partial^{\nu}\big(F_{\nu\lambda}\partial^{\mu}(\delta F)^{\lambda}\big)-\big(\partial^{\mu}(\delta F)^{\lambda}\big)\big(\partial^{\nu}F_{\nu\lambda}\big)

\displaystyle=\eta^{\nu\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}\big(F_{\nu\lambda}\partial^{\mu}(\delta F)^{\lambda}\big)-\big(\partial^{\mu}(\delta F)^{\lambda}\big)\big(\partial_{\nu}\mathchoice{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}\big)

\displaystyle=\eta^{\alpha\nu}\partial_{\nu}\big(F_{\alpha\lambda}\partial^{\mu}(\delta F)^{\lambda}\big)+\big(\partial^{\mu}(\delta F)^{\lambda}\big)(\delta F)_{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\partial_{\nu}\big(\mathchoice{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}\partial^{\mu}(\delta F)^{\lambda}\big)+\frac{1}{2}\big(\partial^{\mu}(\delta F)^{\lambda}\big)(\delta F)_{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2}\big(\partial^{\mu}(\delta F)_{\lambda}\big)(\delta F)^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\partial_{\nu}W^{\mu\nu}+\frac{1}{2}\partial^{\mu}\big((\delta F)^{\alpha}(\delta F)_{\alpha}\big)\qquad\text{where}\quad\boxed{W^{\mu\nu}:=\mathchoice{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}\partial^{\mu}(\delta F)^{\lambda}}

\displaystyle=\partial_{\nu}W^{\mu\nu}+\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\mu\nu}\partial_{\nu}\big((\delta F)^{\alpha}(\delta F)_{\alpha}\big)

\displaystyle=\partial_{\nu}W^{\mu\nu}+\partial_{\nu}V^{\mu\nu}_{(3)}\qquad\text{where}\quad\boxed{V^{\mu\nu}_{(3)}:=\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\mu\nu}\big((\delta F)^{\alpha}(\delta F)_{\alpha}\big)}.

We have now achieved the decomposition

(2.41)

\mathchoice{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}\partial_{\nu}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\nu\lambda}=\partial_{\nu}\big(V^{\mu\nu}_{(1)}+V^{\mu\nu}_{(2)}+V^{\mu\nu}_{(3)}+W^{\mu\nu}\big),

but there are parts of it that are still not symmetric. We need to work with
W^{\mu\nu}
a little more:

\displaystyle W^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}\partial^{\mu}(\delta F)^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\mu\lambda}+\mathchoice{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}\partial^{\lambda}(\delta F)^{\mu}

\displaystyle=V^{\mu\nu}_{(4)}+\partial^{\lambda}\big(\mathchoice{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}(\delta F)^{\mu}\big)-(\delta F)^{\mu}\partial^{\lambda}\mathchoice{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}\qquad\text{where}\quad\boxed{V^{\mu\nu}_{(4)}:=\mathchoice{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\mu\lambda}}

\displaystyle=V^{\mu\nu}_{(4)}+\partial_{\lambda}\big(F^{\nu\lambda}(\delta F)^{\mu}\big)-(\delta F)^{\mu}\partial_{\lambda}F^{\nu\lambda}

\displaystyle=V^{\mu\nu}_{(4)}+\partial_{\lambda}\big(F^{\nu\lambda}(\delta F)^{\mu}\big)-(\delta F)^{\mu}(\delta F)^{\nu}

\displaystyle=V^{\mu\nu}_{(4)}+V^{\mu\nu}_{(5)}+\partial_{\lambda}\big(F^{\nu\lambda}(\delta F)^{\mu}\big)\qquad\text{where}\quad\boxed{V^{\mu\nu}_{(5)}:=-(\delta F)^{\mu}(\delta F)^{\nu}}.

We recognize that
V_{(4)}
is the promised counterpart of
V_{(1)}
, that is,
V_{(1)}^{\mu\nu}+V_{(4)}^{\mu\nu}
is symmetric. Thus (
2.41
) finally turns into

\mathchoice{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{F^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}\partial_{\nu}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\nu\lambda}=\partial_{\nu}\bigg(V^{\mu\nu}_{(1)}+V^{\mu\nu}_{(2)}+V^{\mu\nu}_{(3)}+V^{\mu\nu}_{(4)}+V^{\mu\nu}_{(5)}+\partial_{\lambda}\big(F^{\nu\lambda}(\delta F)^{\mu}\big)\bigg)=\partial_{\nu}\sum_{j=1}^{5}V^{\mu\nu}_{(j)},

where
\partial_{\nu}\partial_{\lambda}\big(F^{\nu\lambda}(\delta F)^{\mu}\big)
vanished due to symmetry of
\partial_{\nu}\partial_{\lambda}
combined with antisymmetry of
F^{\nu\lambda}
. Then we can define
4\pi T^{\mu\nu}_{\mathrm{(BLTP)}}
as the sum of the
V_{(j)}
terms, which is a symmetric expression that yields the formula claimed in the proposition.
∎

By now plugging in the components of
F
as in (
2.23
) into formula (
2.39
) for
(T^{\mu\nu})
, we finally obtain exactly the claimed formula (
2.11
) for
T^{00}=\mathfrak{e}
(see (
2.24
)). Indeed, the individual terms of (
2.39
) for
\mu=\nu=0
come out as

\displaystyle F^{\alpha 0}\mathchoice{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}=\left\lvert\bm{E}\right\rvert^{2},

\displaystyle-\dfrac{\eta^{00}}{4}F_{\alpha\beta}F^{\alpha\beta}=-\dfrac{\left\lvert\bm{E}\right\rvert^{2}}{2}+\dfrac{\left\lvert\bm{B}\right\rvert^{2}}{2},

\displaystyle 2\varkappa^{-2}F^{\alpha 0}\mathchoice{(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}=-2\varkappa^{-2}\bm{E}\cdot\square\bm{E},

\displaystyle\varkappa^{-2}\dfrac{\eta^{00}}{2}F_{\alpha\beta}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\alpha\beta}=\varkappa^{-2}\big(\bm{E}\cdot\square\bm{E}-\bm{B}\cdot\square\bm{B}\big),

\displaystyle-\varkappa^{-2}(\delta F)^{0}(\delta F)^{0}=-\varkappa^{-2}(\nabla\cdot\bm{E})^{2},

\displaystyle\varkappa^{-2}\dfrac{\eta^{00}}{2}(\delta F)^{\alpha}(\delta F)_{\alpha}=\varkappa^{-2}\left(\dfrac{(\nabla\cdot\bm{E})^{2}}{2}-\dfrac{1}{2}\left|\nabla\times\bm{B}-\dfrac{1}{c}\dfrac{\partial\bm{E}}{\partial t}\right|^{2}\right),

whose sum is the right side of (
2.11
).

3.
Electromagnetism in General Relativity

The covariant formulation of electromagnetism in Special Relativity applies promptly to General Relativity. In this section we elaborate on this topic, also explaining how the EM fields couple to gravity via the Einstein equations. Our goal is to derive the GR equations for the spacetime of a static point charge under the Maxwell-Maxwell and Maxwell-BLTP theories of electromagnetism, but we initially let the source term
J
be general in the discussion.

3.1.
Covariant formulation of electromagnetism in GR

Let
\mathcal{M}
be an oriented 4-dimensional Lorentzian manifold with a metric
g
. Let a 1-form field
J\in\bigwedge\nolimits^{1}(\mathcal{M})
be given satisfying
\delta J=0
. Then the
Maxwell equations
and
vacuum law
for two unknown 2-form fields
F,M\in\bigwedge\nolimits^{2}(\mathcal{M})
with source term
J
are by definition the same covariant equations (
2.26
) and (
2.27
) as in the SR case, with
\varkappa=\infty
yielding the conventional Maxwell theory and
0<\varkappa<\infty
the BLTP theory. As before, the equation
\mathrm{d}F=0
implies the local existence of a 1-form field
A\in\bigwedge\nolimits^{1}(\mathcal{M})
such that
F=\mathrm{d}A
, and it is now easy to check that the remaining EM equations are given by the Euler-Lagrange equation of the same Lagrangian
\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{EM}}=\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{EM}}(A)
given in (
2.33
). For this purpose, the proof of Proposition
2.1
simply needs to be adapted with the inclusion of factors of
\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}
and its inverse at the appropriate places, that is, in the Euler-Lagrange equation (
2.34
) itself and everywhere where the coordinate expression (
1.9
) of
\delta
was used. Note how we have been careful in that proof to only apply (
1.8
) to tensors with lower indices and (
1.9
) to those with upper indices, as would be required in a general manifold. We skip the details of this proof.

3.2.
The Hilbert stress-energy tensor

Since our EM theories (with either
\varkappa=\infty
or
0<\varkappa<\infty
) are derived from a Lagrangian
\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{EM}}
, the standard procedure of varying
\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{EM}}
with respect to the inverse metric
(g^{\mu\nu})
can be applied to produce a canonical definition for the
covariant
stress-energy tensor — that is,
(T_{\mu\nu})
as opposed to
(T^{\mu\nu})
— that acts as the source of gravity in GR. More precisely: Fix a 2-form
F=\mathrm{d}A\in\bigwedge\nolimits^{2}(\mathcal{M})
that solves our EM equations, and now consider the Lagrangian
\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{EM}}
given in (
2.38
) to be a function of the inverse metric
g^{-1}\in\mathcal{T}^{2}_{0}(\mathcal{M})
. In particular, components of the differential-form fields
A
and
F
with lower indices are considered independent of
g^{-1}
, while those with at least one raised index depend on it via the operation of index raising. Given an arbitrary open set
U\subseteq\mathcal{M}
, define the
Einstein-Hilbert action

(3.1)

\mathcal{S}_{\text{EH}}(g^{-1};U):=\int_{U}\left(\frac{c^{4}}{16\pi G}R\ \mathrm{vol}_{g}+\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{EM}}\right)=\int_{U}\left(\frac{c^{4}}{16\pi G}R+\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}\right)\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}\ \mathrm{d}^{4}x,

where
G
and
c
are Newton’s gravitational constant and the speed of light, and where the Ricci scalar
R
is as given in (
1.2
).
Consider also a differentiable one-parameter family of inverse metrics
g^{-1}_{(s)}\in\mathcal{T}^{2}_{0}(\mathcal{M})
, all coinciding along the boundary of
U
, with the parameter
s
varying in some small interval
(-\varepsilon,\varepsilon)
. We denote
g_{(0)}^{-1}
simply by
g^{-1}
. For a quantity
f=f(g^{-1},\partial g^{-1},\partial^{2}g^{-1})
that depends on the components
g^{\mu\nu}
of an arbitrary inverse metric and on their first– and second-order derivatives
\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu}
,
\partial_{\rho}\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu}
in some coordinate system, one defines the
variational derivative

\delta f:=\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}s}\bigg|_{s=0}f\big(g^{-1}_{(s)},\partial g^{-1}_{(s)},\partial^{2}g^{-1}_{(s)}\big)=\frac{\partial f}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}\delta g^{\mu\nu}+\frac{\partial f}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}\partial_{\xi}(\delta g^{\mu\nu})+\frac{\partial f}{\partial(\partial_{\rho}\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}\partial_{\rho}\partial_{\xi}(\delta g^{\mu\nu}).

The Chain Rule was used for the last equality; note that
\delta
commutes with coordinate derivatives
\partial_{\xi}
. Then it is well known (see
[
15
]
, p. 85) that the variation of the first term of (
3.1
) produces an integrand containing the Einstein tensor, calculated with respect to the
s=0
metric
g_{(0)}=g
. More precisely:

\delta\int_{U}\frac{c^{4}}{16\pi G}R\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}\ \mathrm{d}^{4}x=\frac{c^{4}}{16\pi G}\int_{U}\left(R_{\mu\nu}-\frac{1}{2}Rg_{\mu\nu}\right)(\delta g^{\mu\nu})\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}\ \mathrm{d}^{4}x

Consequently, the Einstein equations (
1.1
) follow from the action principle
\delta\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{EM}}=0
if one defines the so-called
Hilbert stress-energy tensor

T\in\mathcal{T}_{2}^{0}(\mathcal{M})
by the formula

(3.2)

\delta\int_{U}\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}\ \mathrm{d}^{4}x=:-\frac{1}{2}\int_{U}T_{\mu\nu}\ (\delta g^{\mu\nu})\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}\ \mathrm{d}^{4}x.

This object automatically comes out
symmetric
(
T_{\mu\nu}=T_{\nu\mu}
) and
divergence-free
(
\nabla^{\nu}T_{\mu\nu}=0
), independently from the Einstein equations — see
[
15
]
, p. 91. We now check that, for BLTP theory, it generalizes the special-relativistic
(T^{\mu\nu})
that we had defined in (
2.39
):

Proposition 3.1
.

The components
T_{\mu\nu}
of the Hilbert stress-energy tensor defined by
(
3.2
)
are given in any coordinate system by

(3.3)

T_{\mu\nu}=\frac{1}{c}T_{\mu\nu}^{(1)}+\frac{1}{4\pi}T_{\mu\nu}^{(2)}+\frac{\varkappa^{-2}}{4\pi}T_{\mu\nu}^{(3)}

for

\displaystyle T_{\mu\nu}^{(1)}=g_{\mu\nu}J^{\alpha}A_{\alpha}-J_{\mu}A_{\nu}-J_{\nu}A_{\mu},

\displaystyle T_{\mu\nu}^{(2)}=\mathchoice{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}F_{\alpha\nu}-\frac{g_{\mu\nu}}{4}F_{\alpha\beta}F^{\alpha\beta},

\displaystyle T_{\mu\nu}^{(3)}=\mathchoice{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)_{\alpha\nu}+\mathchoice{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)_{\alpha\mu}-\frac{g_{\mu\nu}}{2}F_{\alpha\beta}(\mathrm{d}\delta F)^{\alpha\beta}-(\delta F)_{\mu}(\delta F)_{\nu}+\frac{g_{\mu\nu}}{2}(\delta F)_{\alpha}(\delta F)^{\alpha}.

Apart from
T^{(1)}
, which is present due to the energy and momentum associated with the motion of the charges modeled by
J
, we see that the remaining terms are exactly as in (
2.39
), only with lowered indices and with
g
replacing
\eta
.

Proof.

We start by finding formulas for derivatives of metric-related terms with respect to the inverse-metric components. They will be used matter-of-factly throughout this proof. To begin, we differentiate the relation
g_{\tau\theta}g^{\theta\rho}=\delta_{\tau}^{\rho}
to get

0=\frac{\partial g_{\tau\theta}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}g^{\theta\rho}+g_{\tau\theta}\frac{\partial g^{\theta\rho}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}=\frac{\partial g_{\tau\theta}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}g^{\theta\rho}+g_{\tau\theta}\delta^{\theta}_{\mu}\delta^{\rho}_{\nu}=\frac{\partial g_{\tau\theta}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}g^{\theta\rho}+g_{\tau\mu}\delta^{\rho}_{\nu},

and we contract with
g_{\rho\sigma}
to find

(3.4)

0=\frac{\partial g_{\tau\theta}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}\delta^{\theta}_{\sigma}+g_{\tau\mu}g_{\nu\sigma}=\frac{\partial g_{\tau\sigma}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}+g_{\tau\mu}g_{\nu\sigma}\quad\Longrightarrow\quad\frac{\partial g_{\tau\sigma}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}=-g_{\tau\mu}g_{\nu\sigma}.

Next, using the well-known
Jacobi formula

\partial_{g_{\mu\nu}}(\det g)=(\det g)g^{\nu\mu}
for the derivative of a determinant, together with the Chain Rule and (
3.4
), we compute

\frac{\partial(\det g)}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}=\frac{\partial(\det g)}{\partial g_{\alpha\beta}}\frac{\partial g_{\alpha\beta}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}=-(\det g)g^{\beta\alpha}g_{\alpha\mu}g_{\nu\beta}=-(\det g)\delta^{\beta}_{\mu}g_{\nu\beta}=-(\det g)g_{\nu\mu}.

Remembering that
\left\lvert g\right\rvert=-\det g
and also considering
g_{\nu\mu}=g_{\mu\nu}
, we rewrite this identity as

(3.5)

\frac{\partial\left\lvert g\right\rvert}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}=-\left\lvert g\right\rvert g_{\mu\nu},

which immediately gives

(3.6)

\frac{\partial\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}\frac{\partial\left\lvert g\right\rvert}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}=-\frac{\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}{2}g_{\mu\nu}.

Then note, again via the Chain Rule, that

\frac{\partial\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}{\partial x^{\xi}}=\frac{\partial\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}{\partial g^{\sigma\tau}}\frac{\partial g^{\sigma\tau}}{\partial x^{\xi}}=-\frac{\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}{2}g_{\sigma\tau}\partial_{\xi}g^{\sigma\tau}=\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\sigma\xi}\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}.

In the last step we used the identity

(3.7)

\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\sigma\xi}=-\frac{1}{2}g_{\sigma\tau}(\partial_{\xi}g^{\sigma\tau}),

whose proof follows from the definition (
1.2
) for the Christoffel symbols:

\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\sigma\xi}=\frac{g^{\sigma\lambda}}{2}(\partial_{\sigma}g_{\lambda\xi}+\partial_{\xi}g_{\lambda\sigma}-\partial_{\lambda}g_{\sigma\xi})=\frac{1}{2}g^{\sigma\lambda}(\partial_{\xi}g_{\sigma\lambda})=-\frac{1}{2}g_{\sigma\lambda}(\partial_{\xi}g^{\sigma\lambda}),

with the last step being a consequence of the product rule applied to
0=\partial_{\xi}(4)=\partial_{\xi}(g_{\sigma\lambda}g^{\sigma\lambda})
.

Having noted down these identities, we can begin expanding
T_{\mu\nu}
from its definition (
3.2
):

\displaystyle\int_{U}T_{\mu\nu}(\delta g^{\mu\nu})\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}\ \mathrm{d}^{4}x

\displaystyle=-2\delta\int_{U}\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}\ \mathrm{d}^{4}x

\displaystyle=-2\int_{U}\bigg(\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}\delta\big(\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}\big)+(\delta\ell_{\mathrm{EM}})\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}\bigg)\ \mathrm{d}^{4}x

\displaystyle=-2\int_{U}\bigg(\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}\frac{\partial\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}(\delta g^{\mu\nu})+\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}(\delta g^{\mu\nu})\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}+\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}\partial_{\xi}(\delta g^{\mu\nu})\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}\bigg)\ \mathrm{d}^{4}x

\displaystyle=-2\int_{U}\bigg(\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}\frac{\partial\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}+\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}-\partial_{\xi}\left(\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}\right)\bigg)(\delta g^{\mu\nu})\ \mathrm{d}^{4}x

\displaystyle=-2\int_{U}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}\frac{\partial\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}+\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}\ -\frac{1}{\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}\partial_{\xi}\left(\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}\right)\bigg)(\delta g^{\mu\nu})\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}\ \mathrm{d}^{4}x,

where integration by parts in the
\xi
variable was used once, as well as the fact that
\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}
only depends on metric-component derivatives up to order 1. Comparing the integrands of the expressions at the start and the end, and considering the arbitrariness of the variation
\delta g_{\mu\nu}
, we find the explicit definition of
T_{\mu\nu}
, valid in any coordinate system:

\displaystyle T_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2}{\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}\frac{\partial\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}-2\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}+\frac{2}{\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}\partial_{\xi}\left(\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}\right)

\displaystyle=-\frac{2}{\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}\left(-\frac{\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}{2}g_{\mu\nu}\right)-2\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}+\frac{2}{\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}\left(\partial_{\xi}\left(\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}\right)\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}+\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}\big(\partial_{\xi}\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}\big)\right)

\displaystyle=g_{\mu\nu}\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}-2\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}+2\partial_{\xi}\left(\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}\right)+2\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\sigma\xi}\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}.

Now consider the coordinate expression of our Lagrangian scalar
\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}
, given in (
2.35
):

\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}=\frac{1}{c}J^{\alpha}A_{\alpha}-\frac{1}{16\pi}g^{\alpha\zeta}g^{\beta\eta}F_{\alpha\beta}F_{\zeta\eta}-\frac{\varkappa^{-2}}{8\pi}g_{\alpha\zeta}(\delta F)^{\alpha}(\delta F)^{\zeta}=\frac{1}{c}\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}^{(1)}-\frac{1}{16\pi}\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}^{(2)}-\frac{\varkappa^{-2}}{8\pi}\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}^{(3)}.

Define accordingly

(3.8)

T_{\mu\nu}=\frac{1}{c}\widetilde{T}_{\mu\nu}^{(1)}-\frac{1}{16\pi}\widetilde{T}_{\mu\nu}^{(2)}-\frac{\varkappa^{-2}}{8\pi}\widetilde{T}_{\mu\nu}^{(3)}

for

(3.9)

\widetilde{T}_{\mu\nu}^{(j)}=g_{\mu\nu}\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}^{(j)}-2\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}^{(j)}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}+2\partial_{\xi}\left(\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}^{(j)}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}\right)+2\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\sigma\xi}\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}^{(j)}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})},\quad j=1,2,3.

The
T^{(j)}_{\mu\nu}
terms as stated in (
3.3
) are mere multiples of the ones with tildes, which are used in this proof simply for convenience:

(3.10)

T^{(1)}_{\mu\nu}=\widetilde{T}^{(1)}_{\mu\nu},\quad T^{(2)}_{\mu\nu}=-\frac{1}{4}\widetilde{T}^{(2)}_{\mu\nu},\quad T^{(3)}_{\mu\nu}=-\frac{1}{2}\widetilde{T}^{(3)}_{\mu\nu}.

We now compute
\widetilde{T}^{(j)}_{\mu\nu}
for
j=2
,
j=3
and
j=1
in this order. To begin, since
\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}^{(2)}
only features
g^{\mu\nu}
terms as opposed to
\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu}
terms, equation (
3.9
) simplifies down to

\displaystyle\widetilde{T}_{\mu\nu}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=g_{\mu\nu}\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}^{(2)}-2\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}^{(2)}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}

\displaystyle=\left(g_{\mu\nu}g^{\alpha\zeta}g^{\beta\eta}F_{\alpha\beta}F_{\zeta\eta}-2\frac{\partial(g^{\alpha\zeta}g^{\beta\eta})}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}F_{\alpha\beta}F_{\zeta\eta}\right)

\displaystyle=g_{\mu\nu}F_{\alpha\beta}F^{\alpha\beta}-2\big(\delta^{\alpha}_{\mu}\delta^{\zeta}_{\nu}g^{\beta\eta}+g^{\alpha\zeta}\delta^{\beta}_{\mu}\delta^{\eta}_{\nu}\big)F_{\alpha\beta}F_{\zeta\eta}

\displaystyle=g_{\mu\nu}F_{\alpha\beta}F^{\alpha\beta}-2g^{\beta\eta}F_{\mu\beta}F_{\nu\eta}-2g^{\alpha\zeta}F_{\alpha\mu}F_{\zeta\nu}

\displaystyle=g_{\mu\nu}F_{\alpha\beta}F^{\alpha\beta}-2g^{\beta\eta}F_{\beta\mu}F_{\eta\nu}-2g^{\alpha\zeta}F_{\alpha\mu}F_{\zeta\nu}

\displaystyle=g_{\mu\nu}F_{\alpha\beta}F^{\alpha\beta}-2\mathchoice{F^{{{\eta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12018pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12018pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.433pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.73785pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{F^{{{\eta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12018pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12018pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.433pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.73785pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{F^{{{\eta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12018pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12018pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.433pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.73785pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{F^{{{\eta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12018pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12018pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.433pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.73785pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}F_{\eta\nu}-2\mathchoice{F^{{{\zeta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.6389pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.6389pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.14375pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.53125pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{F^{{{\zeta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.6389pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.6389pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.14375pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.53125pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{F^{{{\zeta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.6389pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.6389pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.14375pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.53125pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{F^{{{\zeta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.6389pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.6389pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.14375pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.53125pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}F_{\zeta\nu}

\displaystyle=g_{\mu\nu}F_{\alpha\beta}F^{\alpha\beta}-4\mathchoice{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}F_{\alpha\nu}.

Considering (
3.10
), this gives
T^{(2)}_{\mu\nu}
exactly as stated in the proposition. The
j=3
term now will require the bulk of the work, seeing as how the
\delta
operator depends on terms of both types
g^{\mu\nu}
and
\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu}
(we remind the reader that each
\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu}
is considered a variable that is independent from each
g^{\mu\nu}
). First we calculate the following derivatives:

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}^{(3)}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}\big(g_{\alpha\zeta}(\delta F)^{\alpha}(\delta F)^{\zeta}\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial g_{\alpha\zeta}}{g^{\mu\nu}}(\delta F)^{\alpha}(\delta F)^{\zeta}+g_{\alpha\zeta}\frac{\partial(\delta F)^{\alpha}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}(\delta F)^{\zeta}+g_{\alpha\zeta}(\delta F)^{\alpha}\frac{\partial(\delta F)^{\zeta}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}

\displaystyle=-g_{\alpha\mu}g_{\nu\zeta}(\delta F)^{\alpha}(\delta F)^{\zeta}+\frac{\partial(\delta F)^{\alpha}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}(\delta F)_{\alpha}+\frac{\partial(\delta F)^{\zeta}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}(\delta F)_{\zeta}

\displaystyle=-(\delta F)_{\mu}(\delta F)_{\nu}+2\frac{\partial(\delta F)^{\alpha}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}(\delta F)_{\alpha},

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\ell_{\mathrm{EM}}^{(3)}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}\big(g_{\alpha\zeta}(\delta F)^{\alpha}(\delta F)^{\zeta}\big)

\displaystyle=g_{\alpha\zeta}\frac{\partial(\delta F)^{\alpha}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}(\delta F)^{\zeta}+g_{\alpha\zeta}(\delta F)^{\alpha}\frac{\partial(\delta F)^{\zeta}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial(\delta F)^{\alpha}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}(\delta F)_{\alpha}+\frac{\partial(\delta F)^{\zeta}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}(\delta F)_{\zeta}

\displaystyle=2\frac{\partial(\delta F)^{\alpha}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}(\delta F)_{\alpha},

which we plug into the definition (
3.9
) of
\widetilde{T}^{(3)}_{\mu\nu}
to obtain

(3.11)

\displaystyle\widetilde{T}_{\mu\nu}^{(3)}

\displaystyle=g_{\mu\nu}g_{\alpha\zeta}(\delta F)^{\alpha}(\delta F)^{\zeta}-2\left(-(\delta F)_{\mu}(\delta F)_{\nu}+2\frac{\partial(\delta F)^{\alpha}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}(\delta F)_{\alpha}\right)+2\partial_{\xi}\left(2\frac{\partial(\delta F)^{\alpha}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}(\delta F)_{\alpha}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\sigma\xi}\left(2\frac{\partial(\delta F)^{\alpha}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}(\delta F)_{\alpha}\right)

(3.12)

\displaystyle=2(\delta F)_{\mu}(\delta F)_{\nu}+4\frac{\partial(\delta F)^{\alpha}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}\partial_{\xi}(\delta F)_{\alpha}

(3.13)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(g_{\mu\nu}(\delta F)^{\alpha}-4\frac{\partial(\delta F)^{\alpha}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}+4\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\sigma\xi}\frac{\partial(\delta F)^{\alpha}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}+4\partial_{\xi}\left(\frac{\partial(\delta F)^{\alpha}}{\partial(\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu})}\right)\right)(\delta F)_{\alpha}.

To further expand this, we need a formula for
(\delta F)^{\alpha}
showing its explicit dependence on
g^{\mu\nu}
and
\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu}
terms. We have, from (
1.9
),

(3.14)

\displaystyle(\delta F)^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}\partial_{\lambda}\big(\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}F^{\lambda\alpha}\big)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}}\big(\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\sigma\lambda}\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}F^{\lambda\alpha}+\sqrt{\left\lvert g\right\rvert}\partial_{\lambda}F^{\lambda\alpha}\big)

(3.15)

\displaystyle=-\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\sigma\lambda}F^{\lambda\alpha}-\partial_{\lambda}F^{\lambda\alpha}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}g_{\sigma\tau}(\partial_{\lambda}g^{\sigma\tau})F^{\lambda\alpha}-\partial_{\lambda}F^{\lambda\alpha}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}g_{\sigma\tau}(\partial_{\lambda}g^{\sigma\tau})g^{\lambda\zeta}g^{\alpha\eta}F_{\zeta\eta}-\partial_{\lambda}(g^{\lambda\zeta}g^{\alpha\eta}F_{\zeta\eta})

(3.16)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}g_{\sigma\tau}(\partial_{\lambda}g^{\sigma\tau})g^{\lambda\zeta}g^{\alpha\eta}F_{\zeta\eta}-(\partial_{\lambda}g^{\lambda\zeta})g^{\alpha\eta}F_{\zeta\eta}-g^{\lambda\zeta}(\partial_{\lambda}g^{\alpha\eta})F_{\zeta\eta}-g^{\lambda\zeta}g^{\alpha\eta}(\partial_{\lambda}F_{\zeta\eta}).

Now we must differentiate this with respect to
g^{\mu\nu}
and
\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu}
for fixed values of
\mu,\nu,\xi
and plug the results into (
3.11
).

Here we must pause to talk about a subtle, often overlooked issue that arises in such computations: the use of the symmetry
g^{\mu\nu}=g^{\nu\mu}

before
derivatives with respect to
g^{\mu\nu}
are taken. It usually leads to a non-symmetric
(T^{\mu\nu})
, prompting many authors to simply symmetrize the end result without further explanation. We wish to clarify what the problem is and show why this last-step symmetrization fixes it. As an illustration, suppose that we have a symmetric function
f=f(u,w)
— that is,
f(u,w)=f(w,u)
, which implies
\partial_{u}f(u,w)=\partial_{w}f(w,u)
— and we wish to find
(\partial_{u}f)(u,u)
. This symbol means that first
f(u,w)
must be differentiated with respect to
u
, and then the arguments
(u,w)
must be set equal to each other. Let
f
be given as a sum of many symmetric terms, but imagine that we, much like a freshman Calculus student, mistakenly set
u=w
into some of them (but maybe not
all
of them) before finding the
u
-derivative, then we proceeded to differentiate and set
u=w
into the remaining parts. That is, letting
f(u,w)=g(u,w)+h(u,w)
for symmetric
g
and
h
, we originally wished to find

(3.17)

(\partial_{u}f)(u,u)=(\partial_{u}g)(u,u)+(\partial_{u}h)(u,u),

but instead we have first set
u=w
into the
g
part of
f
(but not into the
h
part), then differentiated, and then set
u=w
into the
h
part, producing the different result

(3.18)

\partial_{u}\big(g(u,u)+h(u,w)\big)\bigg|_{u=w}=(\partial_{u}g)(u,u)+(\partial_{w}g)(u,u)+(\partial_{u}h)(u,w)\bigg|_{u=w}=2(\partial_{u}g)(u,u)+(\partial_{u}h)(u,u).

It turns out that our mistake is easy to fix without having to throw away our work: The desired expression (
3.17
) can be recovered from the expression (
3.18
) that we currently have by adding to it the
w
derivative
of the same expression

g(u,u)+h(u,w)
, then dividing by 2 and setting
u=w
:

\displaystyle\frac{\partial_{u}\big(g(u,u)+h(u,w)\big)+\partial_{w}\big(g(u,u)+h(u,w)\big)}{2}\bigg|_{u=w}

\displaystyle=\frac{2(\partial_{u}g)(u,u)+(\partial_{u}h)(u,w)+0+(\partial_{w}h)(u,w)}{2}\bigg|_{u=w}

\displaystyle=\frac{2(\partial_{u}g)(u,u)+(\partial_{u}h)(u,w)+(\partial_{u}h)(w,u)}{2}\bigg|_{u=w}

\displaystyle=(\partial_{u}g)(u,u)+(\partial_{u}h)(u,u).

For a similar reason, when taking derivatives of formula (
3.16
) for
(\delta F)^{\alpha}
with respect to each
g^{\mu\nu}
or
\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu}
term, we will
not
necessarily obtain the correct answer, due to the fact that we have already used the property
g^{\alpha\beta}=g^{\beta\alpha}
at several different places (but not all possible places) when calculating (
3.16
). We used it, for example, in the identity (
3.2
) for
\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\sigma\lambda}
. However, noting that
(\delta F)^{\alpha}
as given in (
3.14
) is manifestly symmetric as a function of all the pairs of variables
(g^{\alpha\beta},g^{\beta\alpha})
and their coordinate derivatives, the remedy is just as explained above: To fix
\partial_{g^{\mu\nu}}(\delta F)^{\alpha}
or
\partial_{\partial_{\xi}g^{\mu\nu}}(\delta F)^{\alpha}
, we must add to it the expression
\partial_{g^{\nu\mu}}(\delta F)^{\alpha}
or
\partial_{\partial_{\xi}g^{\nu\mu}}(\delta F)^{\alpha}
, which has the roles of
\mu
and
\nu
reversed, then divide the result by 2, and then finish applying the symmetry property if needed. This operation will be denoted by the self-explanatory symbol
[+(\mu\leftrightarrow\nu),\div 2,g=g^{\mathrm{T}}]
; for example,

(3g^{\alpha\beta}+4g^{\beta\alpha})(F_{\alpha\mu}F_{\beta\nu})\quad[+(\mu\leftrightarrow\nu),\div 2,g=g^{\mathrm{T}}]\quad=\frac{7g^{\alpha\beta}(F_{\alpha\mu}F_{\beta\nu}+F_{\alpha\nu}F_{\beta\mu})}{2}.

To save the reader’s printer ink, we write this symbol only at the end of long chains of equalities, but it is implied on all previous lines too.

Now we return to the calculation. Differentiating (
3.16
) with respect to
g^{\mu\nu}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\partial(\delta F)_{\alpha}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial g_{\sigma\tau}}{\partial g^{\mu\nu}}(\partial_{\lambda}g^{\sigma\tau})g^{\lambda\zeta}g^{\alpha\eta}F_{\zeta\eta}+\frac{1}{2}g_{\sigma\tau}(\partial_{\lambda}g^{\sigma\tau})\delta^{\lambda}_{\mu}\delta^{\zeta}_{\nu}g^{\alpha\eta}F_{\zeta\eta}+\frac{1}{2}g_{\sigma\tau}(\partial_{\lambda}g^{\sigma\tau})g^{\lambda\zeta}\delta^{\alpha}_{\mu}\delta^{\eta}_{\nu}F_{\zeta\eta}

\displaystyle\qquad-(\partial_{\lambda}g^{\lambda\zeta})\delta^{\alpha}_{\mu}\delta^{\eta}_{\nu}F_{\zeta\eta}-\delta^{\lambda}_{\mu}\delta^{\zeta}_{\nu}(\partial_{\lambda}g^{\alpha\eta})F_{\zeta\eta}-\delta^{\lambda}_{\mu}\delta^{\zeta}_{\nu}g^{\alpha\eta}(\partial_{\lambda}F_{\zeta\eta})-g^{\lambda\zeta}\delta^{\alpha}_{\mu}\delta^{\eta}_{\nu}(\partial_{\lambda}F_{\zeta\eta})

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}g_{\sigma\mu}g_{\tau\nu}(\partial_{\lambda}g^{\sigma\tau})g^{\lambda\zeta}g^{\alpha\eta}F_{\zeta\eta}+\frac{1}{2}g_{\sigma\tau}(\partial_{\mu}g^{\sigma\tau})g^{\alpha\eta}F_{\nu\eta}+\frac{1}{2}g_{\sigma\tau}(\partial_{\lambda}g^{\sigma\tau})g^{\lambda\zeta}\delta^{\alpha}_{\mu}F_{\zeta\nu}

\displaystyle\qquad-(\partial_{\lambda}g^{\lambda\zeta})\delta^{\alpha}_{\mu}F_{\zeta\nu}-(\partial_{\mu}g^{\alpha\eta})F_{\nu\eta}-g^{\alpha\eta}(\partial_{\mu}F_{\nu\eta})-g^{\lambda\zeta}\delta^{\alpha}_{\mu}(\partial_{\lambda}F_{\zeta\nu})

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}g_{\sigma\mu}g_{\tau\nu}(\partial_{\lambda}g^{\sigma\tau})F^{\lambda\alpha}-\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\sigma\mu}g^{\alpha\eta}F_{\nu\eta}-\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\sigma\lambda}\delta^{\alpha}_{\mu}\mathchoice{F^{{{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{F^{{{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{F^{{{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{F^{{{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}-\partial_{\mu}(g^{\alpha\eta}F_{\nu\eta})-\delta^{\alpha}_{\mu}\partial_{\lambda}(g^{\lambda\zeta}F_{\zeta\nu})

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}g_{\sigma\mu}g_{\tau\nu}(\partial_{\lambda}g^{\sigma\tau})F^{\lambda\alpha}+\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\sigma\mu}g^{\alpha\eta}F_{\eta\nu}-\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\sigma\lambda}\delta^{\alpha}_{\mu}\mathchoice{F^{{{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{F^{{{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{F^{{{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{F^{{{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}+\partial_{\mu}(g^{\alpha\eta}F_{\eta\nu})-\delta^{\alpha}_{\mu}\partial_{\lambda}(g^{\lambda\zeta}F_{\zeta\nu})

(3.19)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}(\partial_{\lambda}g_{\mu\nu})F^{\lambda\alpha}+\partial_{\mu}\mathchoice{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}+\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\sigma\mu}\mathchoice{F^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\s {\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{F^{{{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}+\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\sigma\lambda}\mathchoice{F^{{{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{F^{{{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{F^{{{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{F^{{{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][
